CALCULATION
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Joint relation: J = T intersection D
Upper violation: p_joint > min(p_thermo, p_dynamic)
Lower violation: p_joint < max(0, p_thermo + p_dynamic - 1)
Any logic violation: upper_violation OR lower_violation
violation_rate = any_violation_count / n_samples
Comparisons are strict, exactly as requested; no numerical tolerance or post-hoc clipping was applied.
Rates are computed separately for each seed, then summarized with arithmetic mean and sample SD (ddof=1).
Probabilities are raw model outputs from each seed's frozen test_predictions.csv.
